: && /usr/bin/c++ -O3 -DNDEBUG -arch x86_64 -bundle -Wl,-headerpad_max_install_names -o bin/libggml-cpu-zen4.so src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4-feats.dir/ggml-cpu/arch/x86/cpu-feats.cpp.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4.dir/ggml-cpu/ggml-cpu.c.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4.dir/ggml-cpu/ggml-cpu.cpp.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4.dir/ggml-cpu/repack.cpp.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4.dir/ggml-cpu/iqp.cpp.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4.dir/ggml-cpu/hbm.cpp.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4.dir/ggml-cpu/quants.c.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4.dir/ggml-cpu/traits.cpp.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4.dir/ggml-cpu/amx/amx.cpp.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4.dir/ggml-cpu/amx/mmq.cpp.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4.dir/ggml-cpu/binary-ops.cpp.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4.dir/ggml-cpu/unary-ops.cpp.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4.dir/ggml-cpu/vec.cpp.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4.dir/ggml-cpu/ops.cpp.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4.dir/ggml-cpu/arch/x86/quants.c.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml-cpu-zen4.dir/ggml-cpu/arch/x86/repack.cpp.o  -Wl,-rpath,$BUILD/src  src/libggml-base.0.25.3.dylib  -framework Accelerate && :
: && /usr/bin/c++ -O3 -DNDEBUG -arch x86_64 -dynamiclib -Wl,-headerpad_max_install_names -current_version 0.25.3 -o src/libggml.0.25.3.dylib -install_name @rpath/libggml.0.dylib src/CMakeFiles/ggml.dir/ggml-backend-dl.cpp.o src/CMakeFiles/ggml.dir/ggml-backend-reg.cpp.o  -Wl,-rpath,$BUILD/src  src/libggml-base.0.25.3.dylib && :
/opt/homebrew/bin/cmake -E cmake_symlink_library src/libggml.0.25.3.dylib  src/libggml.0.dylib src/libggml.dylib && :
/usr/bin/c++ -DGGML_BACKEND_DL -DGGML_SHARED -I$SRC/src/../include -O3 -DNDEBUG -std=gnu++17 -arch x86_64   -UNDEBUG -MD -MT tests/CMakeFiles/test-backend-ops.dir/test-backend-ops.cpp.o -MF tests/CMakeFiles/test-backend-ops.dir/test-backend-ops.cpp.o.d -o tests/CMakeFiles/test-backend-ops.dir/test-backend-ops.cpp.o -c $SRC/tests/test-backend-ops.cpp
